<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-47507150" data-id="47507150" data-tags="Programming|Gaming" data-score="814" data-comments="277" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47507150" data-time="1774377292">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.xda-developers.com/wine-11-rewrites-linux-runs-windows-games-speed-gains/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://static0.xdaimages.com/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/wm/2025/09/windows-apps-through-wine.jpg?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=crop' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='xda-developers.com' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.xda-developers.com/wine-11-rewrites-linux-runs-windows-games-speed-gains/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Wine 11 rewrites how Linux runs Windows games at kernel with massive speed gains</span><span class='zh'>Wine 11：在内核层重写以显著提升 Linux 上运行 Windows 游戏的速度</span></a><span class="dom">xda-developers.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Wine 11 overhauls kernel interactions to accelerate Windows games on Linux, delivering substantial speedups; it improves compatibility and reduces overhead in system calls translation and graphics handling.</span><span class='zh'>Wine 11 对内核层进行重写，使 Linux 上运行 Windows 游戏的性能显著提升，并提高了兼容性与系统调用翻译、图形处理的效率。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Wine 11 overhauls kernel interactions to accelerate Windows games on Linux, delivering substantial speedups; it improves compatibility and reduces overhead in system calls translation and graphics handling. This move tightens the gap between Linux gaming and Windows performance, potentially expanding Linux gaming adoption.</span><span class='zh'>Wine 11 对内核层进行重写，使 Linux 上运行 Windows 游戏的性能显著提升，并提高了兼容性与系统调用翻译、图形处理的效率。此举缩小了 Linux 与 Windows 在游戏性能上的差距，有望推动 Linux 游戏生态的扩展。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Gaming' style='--c:#a855f7'>Gaming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>7.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>814</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47507150' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>277</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-47508745" data-id="47508745" data-tags="AI" data-score="619" data-comments="416" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47508745" data-time="1774384268">
<a class="thumb" href="https://blog.jakesaunders.dev/is-anybody-else-bored-of-talking-about-ai/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://blog.jakesaunders.dev/is-anybody-else-bored-of-talking-about-ai/img.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='blog.jakesaunders.dev' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.jakesaunders.dev/is-anybody-else-bored-of-talking-about-ai/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Is anybody else bored of talking about AI?</span><span class='zh'>还有人觉得聊 AI 吗？</span></a><span class="dom">blog.jakesaunders.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A reflective essay questions whether the constant AI chatter has worn people down and argues for focusing on practical, real-world impact over hype.</span><span class='zh'>这篇随笔质疑持续的 AI 热议是否让人疲惫，并主张把重点放在现实世界的实际影响而非炒作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A reflective essay questions whether the constant AI chatter has worn people down and argues for focusing on practical, real-world impact over hype. It pushes readers to value concrete use cases, governance, and measurable progress rather than trending headlines.</span><span class='zh'>这篇随笔质疑持续的 AI 热议是否让人疲惫，并主张把重点放在现实世界的实际影响而非炒作。作者鼓励关注具体的使用场景、治理与可衡量的进展，而不是追逐热度新闻。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>7.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>619</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47508745' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>416</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-47508246" data-id="47508246" data-tags="Business" data-score="596" data-comments="431" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47508246" data-time="1774382465">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.githubstatus.com/incidents/kp06czybl7dw" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GitHub is once again down</span><span class='zh'>GitHub 再次宕机</span></a><span class="dom">githubstatus.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GitHub is experiencing an outage again, disrupting core features used by developers worldwide.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub 再次发生宕机，影响全球开发者的日常工作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GitHub is experiencing an outage again, disrupting core features used by developers worldwide. The incident tests how quickly teams can recover, switch to backups, and maintain workflows across dependent tools like CI/CD, package registries, and collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub 再次发生宕机，影响全球开发者的日常工作。事件测试团队如何迅速恢复、回滚，以及与持续集成、包管理和协作工具等依赖系统的韧性。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>353</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47508608' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>187</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-47504112" data-id="47504112" data-tags="Business|Security" data-score="584" data-comments="343" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47504112" data-time="1774366181">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/03/introducing-apple-business-a-new-all-in-one-platform-for-businesses-of-all-sizes/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/03/introducing-apple-business-a-new-all-in-one-platform-for-businesses-of-all-sizes/tile/Apple-Business-hero-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202603241459' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='apple.com' data-c='#0ea5e9' data-tag='Business'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">05</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/03/introducing-apple-business-a-new-all-in-one-platform-for-businesses-of-all-sizes/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple Business</span><span class='zh'>Apple Business：面向各规模企业的一体化平台</span></a><span class="dom">apple.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple introduces Apple Business, an all-in-one platform to streamline device management, security, and app deployment for organizations of all sizes.</span><span class='zh'>苹果推出 Apple Business，一站式平台用于简化企业级设备管理、安全性与应用部署，覆盖各类规模的组织。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple introduces Apple Business, an all-in-one platform to streamline device management, security, and app deployment for organizations of all sizes. By consolidating IT controls and enterprise workflows, it aims to reduce friction for Apple-powered ecosystems in the workplace. The move could reshape enterprise IT purchasing and cross-device collaboration strategies.</span><span class='zh'>苹果推出 Apple Business，一站式平台用于简化企业级设备管理、安全性与应用部署，覆盖各类规模的组织。通过整合 IT 控制和企业工作流，意在降低在工作场景中使用苹果设备的门槛。这一举措可能影响企业 IT 的采购决策及跨设备协作方式。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.sambent.com/microsofts-plan-to-fix-windows-11-is-gaslighting/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.sambent.com/content/images/size/w1200/2026/03/microsoft-plan-fix-windows-11-gaslighting-1.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='sambent.com' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.sambent.com/microsofts-plan-to-fix-windows-11-is-gaslighting/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft&#x27;s &quot;fix&quot; for Windows 11</span><span class='zh'>微软对 Windows 11 的“修复”：挨打后的花束</span></a><span class="dom">sambent.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft continues to roll out patches for Windows 11, but critics argue the changes are cosmetic and don&#x27;t address core reliability or usability issues.</span><span class='zh'>微软继续推出 Windows 11 的补丁，但批评者认为这些改动多为表面修补，无法解决核心的可靠性或可用性问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft continues to roll out patches for Windows 11, but critics argue the changes are cosmetic and don&#x27;t address core reliability or usability issues. The messaging resembles a post-incident PR sprint rather than substantive engineering fixes. For tech readers, this highlights the gap between marketing narratives and real-world OS quality in major updates.</span><span class='zh'>微软继续推出 Windows 11 的补丁，但批评者认为这些改动多为表面修补，无法解决核心的可靠性或可用性问题。外部宣传更像是事后公关冲刺，而非实质工程改进。对技术读者而言，这凸显了大型操作系统更新中营销叙述与实际软件质量之间的差距。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm/issues/24512" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Malicious litellm_init.pth in litellm 1.82.8 PyPI package – credential stealer</span><span class='zh'>litellm 1.82.8 PyPI 包中的恶意 litellm_init.pth：凭据窃取</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A malicious PyPI package for litellm 1.82.8 contains a litellm_init.pth file that loads a credential-stealing module during import.</span><span class='zh'>在 litellm 1.82.8 的 PyPI 包中发现恶意的 litellm_init.pth，导入时会加载窃取凭据的模块。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A malicious PyPI package for litellm 1.82.8 contains a litellm_init.pth file that loads a credential-stealing module during import. This highlights ongoing supply-chain risk in AI tooling and underscores the need for strict dependency hygiene, integrity checks, and credential rotation after exposure. Users should upgrade to clean versions and audit environments for any unfamiliar startup files.</span><span class='zh'>在 litellm 1.82.8 的 PyPI 包中发现恶意的 litellm_init.pth，导入时会加载窃取凭据的模块。此事件暴露了 AI 工具链中的供应链风险，并强调对依赖项进行严格校验、完整性检查以及暴露后轮换凭据的必要性。如有怀疑，应升级到干净版本并审计运行环境中是否存在异常的启动文件。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cg547ljepvzo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Mystery jump in oil trading ahead of Trump post draws scrutiny</span><span class='zh'>特朗普发帖前油价交易出现神秘跳涨，引发关注</span></a><span class="dom">bbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Crude oil markets showed an unexplained rally ahead of a high-profile Trump post, drawing scrutiny from traders and regulators alike.</span><span class='zh'>在特朗普发布重要言论前，原油市场出现难以解释的跳涨，吸引交易员和监管机构的关注。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Crude oil markets showed an unexplained rally ahead of a high-profile Trump post, drawing scrutiny from traders and regulators alike. Analysts debate whether rapid algorithmic trading, order-flow dynamics, or potential insider information drove the move. The episode underscores how political events can instantly ripple through commodity markets.</span><span class='zh'>在特朗普发布重要言论前，原油市场出现难以解释的跳涨，吸引交易员和监管机构的关注。分析师讨论是否因快速的算法交易、订单流动性变化，或内部信息导致了这一下跳。此事凸显政治事件能即时波及大宗商品市场。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm/issues/24512" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tell HN: Litellm 1.82.7 and 1.82.8 on PyPI are compromised</span><span class='zh'>Litellm 1.82.7 与 1.82.8 在 PyPI 上遭篡改</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Two releases of a lightweight LLM client package were compromised on PyPI, enabling malicious code pushes to downstream users.</span><span class='zh'>两个版本的轻量级 LLM 客户端包在 PyPI 的发布被篡改，导致后续用户可能被注入恶意代码。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Two releases of a lightweight LLM client package were compromised on PyPI, enabling malicious code pushes to downstream users. The incident underscores supply-chain risk in Python ecosystems and the importance of checksum validation and pinning versions. Users should audit dependencies, upgrade to clean builds, and verify package integrity.</span><span class='zh'>两个版本的轻量级 LLM 客户端包在 PyPI 的发布被篡改，导致后续用户可能被注入恶意代码。事件凸显 Python 生态的供应链风险，强调校验和版本锁定的重要性。请审计依赖、升级到干净的构建版本，并验证包的完整性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://newsroom.arm.com/blog/introducing-arm-agi-cpu" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Arm AGI CPU</span><span class='zh'>Arm AGI CPU：面向通用人工智能的新一代处理器</span></a><span class="dom">newsroom.arm.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Arm unveils an AGI-oriented CPU design aimed at accelerating general AI workloads across edge and data-center scale.</span><span class='zh'>Arm 面向通用人工智能推出新一代 CPU 设计，意在在边缘和数据中心规模上加速通用 AI 工作负载。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Arm unveils an AGI-oriented CPU design aimed at accelerating general AI workloads across edge and data-center scale. The architecture focuses on high-bandwidth memory, advanced vector math, and security features to support large models with lower power. This signals Arm&#x27;s push to shape the AI compute ecosystem beyond accelerators, integrating AI-friendly cores into mainstream chips.</span><span class='zh'>Arm 面向通用人工智能推出新一代 CPU 设计，意在在边缘和数据中心规模上加速通用 AI 工作负载。该架构强调高带宽内存、高级向量运算以及安全特性，以在更低功耗下支撑大模型。此举标志着 Arm 正在推动 AI 计算生态，不仅依赖加速器，也在主流芯片中整合对 AI 友好的核心。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.emailmd.dev/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Email.md – Markdown to responsive, email-safe HTML</span><span class='zh'>Email.md：将 Markdown 转换为响应式、邮件安全的 HTML</span></a><span class="dom">emailmd.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Email.md provides a conversion tool turning Markdown into HTML that renders consistently across email clients.</span><span class='zh'>Email.md 提供一种将 Markdown 转换为在各大邮件客户端中呈现一致的 HTML 的工具。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Email.md provides a conversion tool turning Markdown into HTML that renders consistently across email clients. It tackles email-inbox quirks like inlined styles and tables for layout, enabling safer, more reliable newsletters and transactional messages. For developers, it&#x27;s a time-saver when shipping marketing emails.</span><span class='zh'>Email.md 提供一种将 Markdown 转换为在各大邮件客户端中呈现一致的 HTML 的工具。它解决了邮件客户端对样式内联、表格布局等的限制造成的问题，从而让新闻简报与交易邮件变得更安全、可靠。对开发者来说，是发布邮件内容时的省时工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ssrajadh/sentrysearch" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Gemini can now natively embed video, so I built sub-second video search</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 现可原生嵌入视频，我实现了亚秒级视频搜索</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gemini now supports native video embedding, enabling richer media integration in AI workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 现已原生支持嵌入视频，方便在 AI 流程中整合多媒体。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gemini now supports native video embedding, enabling richer media integration in AI workflows. The author built a fast video search system leveraging embeddings to index video frames and transcripts, achieving sub-second lookup. This demonstrates how client-side media capabilities can enable near-instant content discovery.</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 现已原生支持嵌入视频，方便在 AI 流程中整合多媒体。作者基于嵌入向量构建了视频搜索系统，对视频帧与文字转写进行索引，实现亚秒级检索，展示了前端对多媒体内容进行近乎即时检索的潜力。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">17</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://danielhomola.com/m%20&amp;%20e/ai/your-bridge-to-wealth-is-being-pulled-up/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The bridge to wealth is being pulled up with AI</span><span class='zh'>AI抬高了致富之路的门槛</span></a><span class="dom">danielhomola.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI is reshaping how people build wealth by enabling new models of automation, entrepreneurship, and platform-enabled investing.</span><span class='zh'>AI正在重塑致富路径，通过自动化、创业和平台化投资等新模式降低财富门槛，同时也在重塑传统职业阶梯。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI is reshaping how people build wealth by enabling new models of automation, entrepreneurship, and platform-enabled investing. While AI lowers barriers to value creation, it also reorders traditional career ladders, pushing many toward AI-enabled ventures and ownership rather than straight wages.</span><span class='zh'>AI正在重塑致富路径，通过自动化、创业和平台化投资等新模式降低财富门槛，同时也在重塑传统职业阶梯。AI可能让人更易创造价值，但也促使不少人从单纯靠工资的工作转向基于AI的创业和资产所有权。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://smu160.github.io/posts/missile-defense-is-np-complete/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Missile defense is NP-complete</span><span class='zh'>导弹防御是 NP 完全问题</span></a><span class="dom">smu160.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post argues that certain missile defense planning problems are NP-complete, implying intrinsic computational limits for optimal real-time solutions.</span><span class='zh'>文中指出某些导弹防御规划问题属于 NP 完全，暗示在实时求解中存在本质的计算极限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post argues that certain missile defense planning problems are NP-complete, implying intrinsic computational limits for optimal real-time solutions. The takeaway is that heuristics and approximations remain essential in defense system design.</span><span class='zh'>文中指出某些导弹防御规划问题属于 NP 完全，暗示在实时求解中存在本质的计算极限。结论是，在防御系统设计中仍需依赖启发式与近似方法。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A 2016 comparison shows Ripgrep outperforms traditional search tools on large codebases thanks to Rust-based internals and smarter regex handling.</span><span class='zh'>2016 年的比较显示 Ripgrep 在大型代码库中明显快于 grep、ag、git grep 等工具，得益于 Rust 实现和更高效的正则处理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A 2016 comparison shows Ripgrep outperforms traditional search tools on large codebases thanks to Rust-based internals and smarter regex handling. It helped popularize fast, developer-friendly search in modern toolchains.</span><span class='zh'>2016 年的比较显示 Ripgrep 在大型代码库中明显快于 grep、ag、git grep 等工具，得益于 Rust 实现和更高效的正则处理。这推动了现代工具链对快速搜索的追求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/anemll/status/2035901335984611412" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>iPhone 17 Pro Demonstrated Running a 400B LLM</span><span class='zh'>iPhone 17 Pro 展示本地运行 400B 参数的 LLM</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An iPhone 17 Pro demonstration shows a 400-billion-parameter LLM running locally on-device, signaling advances in edge AI and privacy-preserving inference.</span><span class='zh'>一段演示显示 iPhone 17 Pro 能在本地设备上运行 400B 参数的 LLM，标志边缘 AI 的进展与隐私保护推理能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An iPhone 17 Pro demonstration shows a 400-billion-parameter LLM running locally on-device, signaling advances in edge AI and privacy-preserving inference. The feat highlights model compression, efficient on-device runtimes, and the potential shift away from cloud-only AI.</span><span class='zh'>一段演示显示 iPhone 17 Pro 能在本地设备上运行 400B 参数的 LLM，标志边缘 AI 的进展与隐私保护推理能力。此成就凸显模型压缩、边缘运行效率的提升，以及 AI 由云端依赖向本地推理的潜在转变。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.fcc.gov/document/fcc-updates-covered-list-include-foreign-made-consumer-routers" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=fcc.gov&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>fcc.gov</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">24</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.fcc.gov/document/fcc-updates-covered-list-include-foreign-made-consumer-routers" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>FCC updates covered list to include foreign-made consumer routers</span><span class='zh'>FCC 将外国制造的消费路由器纳入覆盖清单</span></a><span class="dom">fcc.gov</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The FCC expands the Covered Equipment list to include foreign-made consumer routers, tightening security requirements for home networking gear sold in the U.S.</span><span class='zh'>FCC 将外国制造的消费路由器纳入覆盖清单，强化对美国市场网络设备的安全要求。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The FCC expands the Covered Equipment list to include foreign-made consumer routers, tightening security requirements for home networking gear sold in the U.S. The move could affect manufacturers, supply chains, and consumer privacy as compliance costs become clearer.</span><span class='zh'>FCC 将外国制造的消费路由器纳入覆盖清单，强化对美国市场网络设备的安全要求。此举可能影响厂商、供应链与合规成本，进而关系到家庭网络的隐私与安全。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lnav.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Log File Viewer for the Terminal</span><span class='zh'>终端日志查看器</span></a><span class="dom">lnav.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>lnav is a powerful terminal-based log viewer that aggregates and parses multiple log formats, offering fast search, color highlighting, and time-based filtering.</span><span class='zh'>lnav 是一个强大的终端日志查看工具，能聚合并解析多种日志格式，支持快速搜索、颜色高亮和基于时间的过滤。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>lnav is a powerful terminal-based log viewer that aggregates and parses multiple log formats, offering fast search, color highlighting, and time-based filtering. It makes triaging issues across services easier by providing structured views and cross-reference across files without leaving the terminal.</span><span class='zh'>lnav 是一个强大的终端日志查看工具，能聚合并解析多种日志格式，支持快速搜索、颜色高亮和基于时间的过滤。它通过在同一界面中跨文件浏览和关联信息，简化了跨服务的问题排查与调试。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Autoresearch on an old research idea revisits a long-standing question using modern tooling to gather data, test hypotheses, and track results.</span><span class='zh'>本文展示了对一个长期存在的研究想法进行自动化再研究，借助现代工具进行数据收集、假设测试与结果跟踪。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Autoresearch on an old research idea revisits a long-standing question using modern tooling to gather data, test hypotheses, and track results. The piece offers practical lessons on automation, reproducibility, and how iterative, self-driven inquiry changes the pace of scientific exploration.</span><span class='zh'>本文展示了对一个长期存在的研究想法进行自动化再研究，借助现代工具进行数据收集、假设测试与结果跟踪。通过案例，讨论自动化、可重复性，以及自驱式研究如何改变科研的节奏。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PC Gamer curates a selection of RSS readers in a long, 37MB feature. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GitHub appears to be facing reliability issues despite being a core platform for developers, raising concerns about SLOs and uptime for massive code and CI pipelines.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub 作为开发者的核心平台，正面临可靠性问题，令大规模代码托管与持续集成流水线的可用性成为焦点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GitHub appears to be facing reliability issues despite being a core platform for developers, raising concerns about SLOs and uptime for massive code and CI pipelines. The outages stress the need for stronger resilience planning and incident response.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub 作为开发者的核心平台，正面临可靠性问题，令大规模代码托管与持续集成流水线的可用性成为焦点。事件暴露出在弹性设计和应急响应方面的不足，强调需要更强的容错能力。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Two pilots died after a collision between an aircraft and a ground vehicle at LaGuardia.</span><span class='zh'>拉瓜迪亚机场发生飞机与地面车辆相撞，造成两名飞行员死亡。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Two pilots died after a collision between an aircraft and a ground vehicle at LaGuardia. The incident spotlights airport surface safety and the need for robust protocols and automation to prevent such tragedies.</span><span class='zh'>拉瓜迪亚机场发生飞机与地面车辆相撞，造成两名飞行员死亡。事件凸显机场地面安全与防止此类事故的流程与自动化的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://indieweb.org/POSSE" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>POSSE – Publish on your Own Site, Syndicate Elsewhere</span><span class='zh'>POSSE：先在自家站点发布，再在外部平台聚合</span></a><span class="dom">indieweb.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>POSSE is the principle of publishing on your own site first, then syndicating copies to external platforms.</span><span class='zh'>POSSE 的原则是在自有站点优先发布，再将复制内容聚合到外部平台。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>POSSE is the principle of publishing on your own site first, then syndicating copies to external platforms. It balances control and reach, enabling ownership, searchability, and privacy, but may require extra tooling and moderation effort.</span><span class='zh'>POSSE 的原则是在自有站点优先发布，再将复制内容聚合到外部平台。此举在控制与扩散之间取得平衡，提升内容所有权、可检索性与隐私，但也需要额外的工具和治理工作。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/S016517652200283X" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#60a5fa' data-tag='Education'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=sciencedirect.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>sciencedirect.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/S016517652200283X" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Student beauty and grades under in-person and remote teaching</span><span class='zh'>面授与远程教学下，学生外貌与成绩的关系</span></a><span class="dom">sciencedirect.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A study compares how student appearance correlates with grades under in-person versus remote teaching.</span><span class='zh'>一项研究比较了在面授与远程教学情境下，学生外貌与成绩的相关性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A study compares how student appearance correlates with grades under in-person versus remote teaching. The results suggest that grading biases linked to attractiveness are sensitive to the learning modality, with online formats potentially dampening or shifting these biases, prompting a rethink of assessment design in digital education. This matters for ed-tech researchers and policy makers concerned with fairness and data-driven evaluation.</span><span class='zh'>一项研究比较了在面授与远程教学情境下，学生外貌与成绩的相关性。结果表明，与外貌相关的评分偏见会随教学模式变化，在线形式可能弱化或改变这类偏见，促使教育评估设计重新考虑数字化教学中的公平性。此发现对教育科技研究者与政策制定者关注的公平性和基于数据的评估具有现实意义。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bramcohen.com/p/manyana" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The future of version control</span><span class='zh'>版本控制的未来</span></a><span class="dom">bramcohen.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Visionaries describe the next phase of version control beyond Git-style systems, spotlighting peer-to-peer collaboration, cryptographic integrity, and scalable, offline-friendly workflows.</span><span class='zh'>讨论了超越现有版本控制系统的新范式，强调点对点协作、加密完整性和可离线扩展的工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Visionaries describe the next phase of version control beyond Git-style systems, spotlighting peer-to-peer collaboration, cryptographic integrity, and scalable, offline-friendly workflows. The takeaway is that new paradigms could change how teams coordinate, verify history, and scale development. For technologists, this signals a shift in toolchains and trust conventions.</span><span class='zh'>讨论了超越现有版本控制系统的新范式，强调点对点协作、加密完整性和可离线扩展的工作流。核心在于新机制可能改变团队的协作、历史验证和开发扩展的方式。对开发者来说，这预示着工具链与信任体系的变革。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A discreet retrofit of a standalone apartment intercom to integrate with Apple HomeKit enables automations and remote control without replacing the entire system.</span><span class='zh'>本文记录了如何低调为公寓对讲机做改造，使其能接入 Apple HomeKit，从而在家庭自动化中实现通过 Home.app 的控制与场景触发。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A discreet retrofit of a standalone apartment intercom to integrate with Apple HomeKit enables automations and remote control without replacing the entire system.</span><span class='zh'>本文记录了如何低调为公寓对讲机做改造，使其能接入 Apple HomeKit，从而在家庭自动化中实现通过 Home.app 的控制与场景触发。强调在利用现有设备构建生态时需要关注安全、稳定性以及对硬件的潜在风险和保修影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://stevekrouse.com/precision" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Reports of code&#x27;s death are greatly exaggerated</span><span class='zh'>关于代码死亡的传闻被大大夸大</span></a><span class="dom">stevekrouse.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Code isn&#x27;t dead; AI tools will augment but not replace human software craftsmen.</span><span class='zh'>尽管有 AI 辅助写码的趋势，代码并未消亡。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Code isn&#x27;t dead; AI tools will augment but not replace human software craftsmen. The piece argues that precise, maintainable code, rigorous testing, and clear design remain essential as automation shifts routine work. In practice, expect a continued emphasis on tooling that preserves correctness and developer control.</span><span class='zh'>尽管有 AI 辅助写码的趋势，代码并未消亡。高质量、可维护的代码、严谨的测试和明确的设计仍然是关键，因为自动化会改变工作方式但需要人类把控。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.birkey.co/2026-03-22-why-i-love-nixos.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why I love NixOS</span><span class='zh'>我为什么爱上 NixOS</span></a><span class="dom">birkey.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author extols NixOS for reproducible configurations, declarative systems, and isolated packages that reduce drift.</span><span class='zh'>作者盛赞 NixOS 的可重复配置、声明式系统和包隔离性，显著降低环境漂移。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author extols NixOS for reproducible configurations, declarative systems, and isolated packages that reduce drift. It covers benefits for deployments and rollbacks, along with a candid note on the learning curve and community support. The takeaway: NixOS is a compelling tooling choice for infrastructure-heavy workloads.</span><span class='zh'>作者盛赞 NixOS 的可重复配置、声明式系统和包隔离性，显著降低环境漂移。文中还谈及部署与回滚的好处，以及较陡的学习曲线和社区支持。核心结论是在基础设施密集型场景下，NixOS 是一个值得考虑的工具选择。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.joanwestenberg.com/collaboration-is-bullshit/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>“Collaboration” is bullshit</span><span class='zh'>「合作」其实是扯淡</span></a><span class="dom">joanwestenberg.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author questions the value of constant collaboration and argues that it can slow progress, amplify conflict, and dilute accountability.</span><span class='zh'>作者质疑常态化的协作价值，认为它可能拖慢进度、放大冲突并削弱问责。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author questions the value of constant collaboration and argues that it can slow progress, amplify conflict, and dilute accountability. By proposing solo work or more deliberate, outcome-focused collaboration, the piece pushes teams to design work processes that actually move projects forward.</span><span class='zh'>作者质疑常态化的协作价值，认为它可能拖慢进度、放大冲突并削弱问责。文中提出更偏向个人完成的工作方式或更有目的性的协作结构，促使团队设计真正推动项目前进的工作流程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/danveloper/flash-moe" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Flash-MoE: Running a 397B Parameter Model on a Laptop</span><span class='zh'>Flash-MoE：在笔记本上运行397B参数模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Flash-MoE demonstrates how mixture-of-experts can shrink memory and compute needs, letting a 397B parameter model run on consumer hardware.</span><span class='zh'>展示了混合专家（MoE）通过稀疏计算在普通硬件上运行大型模型的可能性，使397B参数模型在笔记本上落地成为现实。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Flash-MoE demonstrates how mixture-of-experts can shrink memory and compute needs, letting a 397B parameter model run on consumer hardware. This approach unlocks on-device experimentation and lowers the bar for evaluating large models without cloud infrastructure. Expect more on-device AI prototyping and edge-friendly deployments.</span><span class='zh'>展示了混合专家（MoE）通过稀疏计算在普通硬件上运行大型模型的可能性，使397B参数模型在笔记本上落地成为现实。此举降低了评估大模型对云资源的依赖，促进本地化实验与边缘部署的发展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/localstack/localstack" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Local Stack Archived their GitHub repo and requires an account to run</span><span class='zh'>LocalStack 将仓库归档，运行需账户</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LocalStack has archived its GitHub repository and now requires you to sign in to run the software locally.</span><span class='zh'>LocalStack 已将其 GitHub 仓库归档，现在运行需要账户登录。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LocalStack has archived its GitHub repository and now requires you to sign in to run the software locally. This marks a shift from open development toward a gated model, potentially affecting developers who rely on a free, self-hosted AWS-like environment. The move invites questions about accessibility and sustainability of community-focused open-source projects.</span><span class='zh'>LocalStack 已将其 GitHub 仓库归档，现在运行需要账户登录。这标志着从开放开发向受限访问的转变，可能影响依赖免费本地 AWS 模拟环境的开发者。此举也引发关于社区开源项目可访问性与可持续性的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cyber.mil/stigs/downloads" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cyber.mil serving file downloads using TLS certificate which expired 3 days ago</span><span class='zh'>Cyber.mil 使用已过期的 TLS 证书提供下载</span></a><span class="dom">cyber.mil</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An official Cyber.mil download service was served over TLS with a certificate that expired three days earlier.</span><span class='zh'>Cyber.mil 的下载服务在 TLS 加密中使用了三天前就到期的证书，暴露了信任与中间人攻击的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An official Cyber.mil download service was served over TLS with a certificate that expired three days earlier. The lapse risks trust and introduces MITM exposure, highlighting gaps in certificate lifecycle management for critical infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>Cyber.mil 的下载服务在 TLS 加密中使用了三天前就到期的证书，暴露了信任与中间人攻击的风险。此事凸显关键基础设施的证书生命周期管理薄弱，需要改进。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A study finds that security fatigue may erode digital defenses as users tire of strict practices.</span><span class='zh'>研究发现安全疲劳可能削弱数字防御，因为用户对严格的安全做法感到疲惫。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A study finds that security fatigue may erode digital defenses as users tire of strict practices. The findings argue for better UX, stronger defaults, and more practical security messaging.</span><span class='zh'>研究发现安全疲劳可能削弱数字防御，因为用户对严格的安全做法感到疲惫。研究建议通过更友好的用户体验、更强的默认设置和更实用的安全信息来改善。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/jer-irl/threadprocs" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Threadprocs – executables sharing one address space (0-copy pointers)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Threadprocs — 在同一地址空间共享可执行文件（零拷贝指针）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Threadprocs enables multiple executables to share a single address space, enabling zero-copy data sharing and fast IPC.</span><span class='zh'>Threadprocs 允许多个可执行文件共用一个地址空间，实现零拷贝的数据共享与快速进程间通信。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Threadprocs enables multiple executables to share a single address space, enabling zero-copy data sharing and fast IPC. The approach challenges traditional process isolation and could influence future runtime and OS design.</span><span class='zh'>Threadprocs 允许多个可执行文件共用一个地址空间，实现零拷贝的数据共享与快速进程间通信。这一思路挑战传统进程隔离，可能影响未来的运行时与操作系统设计。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Haskell&#x27;s Dataframe project reaches 1.0.0.0, signaling a stable API for tabular data manipulation in Haskell with a focus on type-safety and performance.</span><span class='zh'>Haskell 的 Dataframe 项目发布 1.0.0.0，标志着用于处理表格数据的 API 达到稳定阶段，强调类型安全与性能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Haskell&#x27;s Dataframe project reaches 1.0.0.0, signaling a stable API for tabular data manipulation in Haskell with a focus on type-safety and performance. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://tedium.co/2026/02/25/vibe-coded-email-spam/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>They’re vibe-coding spam now</span><span class='zh'>垃圾邮件现在在做氛围编码</span></a><span class="dom">tedium.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Spam is increasingly using vibe-coded signals to tailor messages to recipients&#x27; identities and contexts, aiming to bypass filters and boost engagement.</span><span class='zh'>垃圾邮件正越来越多地使用氛围编码来针对收件人进行个性化定制，以绕过过滤器并提升投递成功率。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Spam is increasingly using vibe-coded signals to tailor messages to recipients&#x27; identities and contexts, aiming to bypass filters and boost engagement. The trend shows attackers exploiting cultural cues and sentiment to improve deliverability.</span><span class='zh'>垃圾邮件正越来越多地使用氛围编码来针对收件人进行个性化定制，以绕过过滤器并提升投递成功率。此趋势揭示攻击者在提升可达性时利用文化线索和情感共鸣的手段。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://socket.dev/blog/trivy-under-attack-again-github-actions-compromise" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Trivy under attack again: Widespread GitHub Actions tag compromise secrets</span><span class='zh'>Trivy 再度受攻击：GitHub Actions 标签广泛暴露密钥</span></a><span class="dom">socket.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Trivy is again under fire after attackers exploited compromised GitHub Actions tags to steal secrets.</span><span class='zh'>Trivy 再次受攻击，攻击者利用被妥协的 GitHub Actions 标签窃取密钥。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Trivy is again under fire after attackers exploited compromised GitHub Actions tags to steal secrets. The incident underscores how software supply chain tools can be weaponized through CI/CD workflows, prompting teams to pin trusted versions, audit action usage, and rotate leaked credentials.</span><span class='zh'>Trivy 再次受攻击，攻击者利用被妥协的 GitHub Actions 标签窃取密钥。这凸显供应链工具在 CI/CD 流水线中的潜在攻击点，促使团队固定可信版本、审计 Actions 使用并轮换泄露的凭证。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dune3d/dune3d" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Dune3d: A parametric 3D CAD application</span><span class='zh'>Dune3d：一个参数化的3D CAD应用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Dune3d is an open-source parametric 3D CAD project that lets users define and modify geometry with parameters and constraints.</span><span class='zh'>Dune3d 是一个开源的参数化3D CAD项目，允许通过参数和约束来定义和修改几何形状。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Dune3d is an open-source parametric 3D CAD project that lets users define and modify geometry with parameters and constraints. The project exemplifies how modular, scriptable CAD tooling could complement or challenge heavyweight CAD suites.</span><span class='zh'>Dune3d 是一个开源的参数化3D CAD项目，允许通过参数和约束来定义和修改几何形状。该项目展示了轻量、可脚本化的 CAD 工具如何挑战主流工具并实现更快速的试验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://emi-h.com/articles/a-case-against-currying.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A case against currying</span><span class='zh'>对柯里化的反对意见</span></a><span class="dom">emi-h.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Argues that currying can harm readability and debugging in real-world code, especially as function chains grow.</span><span class='zh'>指出在实际代码中，柯里化可能降低可读性和调试性，尤其当函数链日益复杂时。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Argues that currying can harm readability and debugging in real-world code, especially as function chains grow. The piece advocates pragmatic function design and cautions against overusing point-free styles.</span><span class='zh'>指出在实际代码中，柯里化可能降低可读性和调试性，尤其当函数链日益复杂时。提倡更务实的函数设计，警惕过度使用点-free 风格。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.connorjdavis.com/p/intuitions-for-transformer-circuits" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Intuitions for Tranformer Circuits</span><span class='zh'>Transformer 电路的直觉</span></a><span class="dom">connorjdavis.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Offers intuitive takes on how transformer circuits operate in AI interpretability, focusing on recurring circuit motifs across layers and how attention, residuals, and MLPs combine to form representations.</span><span class='zh'>本文给出对 Transformer 电路的直觉性解读，聚焦跨层的可重复电路模组以及注意力、残差和 MLP 如何共同构成表征。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Offers intuitive takes on how transformer circuits operate in AI interpretability, focusing on recurring circuit motifs across layers and how attention, residuals, and MLPs combine to form representations. It highlights practical insights for diagnosing failures and building more robust transformers.</span><span class='zh'>本文给出对 Transformer 电路的直觉性解读，聚焦跨层的可重复电路模组以及注意力、残差和 MLP 如何共同构成表征。提供诊断故障与提升模型鲁棒性的实用洞见，帮助读者在设计和分析 Transformer 时更有把握。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://searchengineland.com/walmart-chatgpt-checkout-converted-worse-472071" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Walmart: ChatGPT checkout converted 3x worse than website</span><span class='zh'>沃尔玛：ChatGPT 结账转化率比官网低三倍</span></a><span class="dom">searchengineland.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A test of ChatGPT-powered checkout at a major retailer shows conversions were three times worse than the regular checkout flow.</span><span class='zh'>在一家大型零售商的结账场景中，ChatGPT 驱动的结账流程的转化率比常规网站结账低三倍。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A test of ChatGPT-powered checkout at a major retailer shows conversions were three times worse than the regular checkout flow. The result underscores that conversational AI must be carefully designed for shopping tasks to avoid frictions, even as AI promises convenience.</span><span class='zh'>在一家大型零售商的结账场景中，ChatGPT 驱动的结账流程的转化率比常规网站结账低三倍。该结果提醒零售商在引入对话式AI时，需要显式优化购物任务的流畅性，避免引入额外的操作摩擦。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://geajs.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>JavaScript Is Enough</span><span class='zh'>JavaScript 就够用</span></a><span class="dom">geajs.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>JavaScript Is Enough argues that JS remains a remarkably capable platform for most web tasks and beyond, thanks to its ecosystem, tooling, and ubiquity.</span><span class='zh'>主张 JavaScript 已足以应对大多数 Web 任务，得益于丰富的生态、工具链与广泛应用范围。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>JavaScript Is Enough argues that JS remains a remarkably capable platform for most web tasks and beyond, thanks to its ecosystem, tooling, and ubiquity. The piece challenges language-purity anxieties and invites readers to lean into pragmatic, monolingual development.</span><span class='zh'>主张 JavaScript 已足以应对大多数 Web 任务，得益于丰富的生态、工具链与广泛应用范围。文章挑战对语言纯粹性的执着，提倡以务实为先的开发思路。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Describes achieving 25 Gbps over standard 10 GbE Direct Attach Copper by optimizing PHY, cabling, and perhaps signaling techniques. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.da.vidbuchanan.co.uk/blog/dram-emfi.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Can you get root with only a cigarette lighter? (2024)</span><span class='zh'>只用打火机就能拿到 root 吗？（2024）</span></a><span class="dom">da.vidbuchanan.co.uk</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers revisit a surprising hardware attack vector and show that a common cigarette lighter can act as a crude fault-injection tool to gain root access on a device.</span><span class='zh'>研究者重新探讨了一个出人意料的硬件攻击路径，展示了常见打火机也可作为粗糙的故障注入工具，从而在某些设备上获得 root 权限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers revisit a surprising hardware attack vector and show that a common cigarette lighter can act as a crude fault-injection tool to gain root access on a device. The write-up highlights how small, inexpensive hardware can create power transients or other disturbances that undermine security, underscoring the ongoing risk to embedded systems.</span><span class='zh'>研究者重新探讨了一个出人意料的硬件攻击路径，展示了常见打火机也可作为粗糙的故障注入工具，从而在某些设备上获得 root 权限。该文章强调廉价小型硬件也会造成电源瞬变等干扰，暴露嵌入式系统安全的脆弱性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/antithesishq/bombadil" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bombadil: Property-based testing for web UIs</span><span class='zh'>Bombadil：Antithesis 的网页 UI 属性测试工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Bombadil brings property-based testing to front-end UI testing, generating diverse interaction sequences to expose edge cases that example tests miss.</span><span class='zh'>Bombadil 为前端 UI 引入属性测试，通过自动生成各种交互序列来揭示常规测试难以覆盖的边界问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Bombadil brings property-based testing to front-end UI testing, generating diverse interaction sequences to expose edge cases that example tests miss. It emphasizes automated shrinking of failing runs to minimal reproducing cases and integrates with common web stacks, helping teams catch flaky behavior before release.</span><span class='zh'>Bombadil 为前端 UI 引入属性测试，通过自动生成各种交互序列来揭示常规测试难以覆盖的边界问题。它还能对失败用例进行最小化缩减，便于定位复现，并与主流前端栈对接，帮助团队在发布前发现易出错的行为。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://iev.ee/blog/the-quadratic-problem-nobody-fixed/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=iev.ee&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>iev.ee</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">104</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://iev.ee/blog/the-quadratic-problem-nobody-fixed/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Finding all regex matches has always been O(n²)</span><span class='zh'>寻找所有正则匹配的时间复杂度一直是 O(n²)</span></a><span class="dom">iev.ee</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Finding all regex matches can deteriorate to quadratic time in worst cases, a long-standing performance pitfall in many engines.</span><span class='zh'>在最坏情况下，寻找所有正则匹配的时间复杂度可能退化为平方级。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Finding all regex matches can deteriorate to quadratic time in worst cases, a long-standing performance pitfall in many engines. The post outlines why this problem persists and offers practical approaches to mitigate it in real-world use.</span><span class='zh'>在最坏情况下，寻找所有正则匹配的时间复杂度可能退化为平方级。本文解释了这一问题为何长期未被根本解决，并给出在实际场景中缓解的可操作方法。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>173</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47443903' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>44</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47454127" data-id="47454127" data-tags="Programming|DevOps" data-score="124" data-comments="44" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47454127" data-time="1774012911">
<a class="thumb" href="https://neon.com/blog/ctrl-c-in-psql-gives-me-the-heebie-jeebies" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://neondatabase.wpenginepowered.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/neon-ctrl-c.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='neon.com' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">105</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://neon.com/blog/ctrl-c-in-psql-gives-me-the-heebie-jeebies" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The way CTRL-C in Postgres CLI cancels queries is incredibly hack-y</span><span class='zh'>PostgreSQL CLI 的 Ctrl-C 取消查询实现极其 hacky</span></a><span class="dom">neon.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Postgres&#x27; psql uses an unusually hacky method to cancel running queries via Ctrl-C, which can leave transactions in limbo and occasionally trigger inconsistent client-server state.</span><span class='zh'>psql 在按 Ctrl-C 取消正在执行的查询时采用一种相当 hacky 的实现，可能让事务进入悬而未决的状态，甚至引发客户端与服务器之间的不一致。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Postgres&#x27; psql uses an unusually hacky method to cancel running queries via Ctrl-C, which can leave transactions in limbo and occasionally trigger inconsistent client-server state.</span><span class='zh'>psql 在按 Ctrl-C 取消正在执行的查询时采用一种相当 hacky 的实现，可能让事务进入悬而未决的状态，甚至引发客户端与服务器之间的不一致。文章强调取消机制的脆弱性，以及为开发者和运维人员带来更清晰、无竞争条件的协议的必要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://emerge-lab.github.io/papers/an-unsolicited-guide-to-good-research.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>An unsolicited guide to being a researcher [pdf]</span><span class='zh'>成为研究者的随手指南（PDF）</span></a><span class="dom">emerge-lab.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An Unsolicited Guide to Being a Researcher offers practical, no-nonsense advice on conducting robust, ethical research, from formulating questions to documenting methods and sharing results.</span><span class='zh'>这份《成为研究者的随手指南》给出务实、无废话的研究建议，覆盖从如何提出研究问题到记录方法、分享结果的全过程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An Unsolicited Guide to Being a Researcher offers practical, no-nonsense advice on conducting robust, ethical research, from formulating questions to documenting methods and sharing results. It emphasizes reproducibility, critical thinking, and the social dynamics of collaboration for lasting impact.</span><span class='zh'>这份《成为研究者的随手指南》给出务实、无废话的研究建议，覆盖从如何提出研究问题到记录方法、分享结果的全过程。强调可重复性、批判性思维，以及在协作和学术共同体中的伦理与责任，帮助研究者提升长期影响力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jtsylve.blog/post/2026/03/18/PSpice-Encryption-Weakness" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A Copy-Paste Bug That Broke PSpice AES-256 Encryption</span><span class='zh'>拷贝粘贴错误导致 PSpice AES-256 加密失效</span></a><span class="dom">jtsylve.blog</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A small copy-paste bug in PSpice&#x27;s AES-256 encryption routine breaks expected cryptographic behavior, potentially causing incorrect ciphertext or decryption failure.</span><span class='zh'>在 PSpice 的 AES-256 加密实现中出现的一个拷贝粘贴错误打乱了原本的加密行为，可能导致密文错误或解密失败。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A small copy-paste bug in PSpice&#x27;s AES-256 encryption routine breaks expected cryptographic behavior, potentially causing incorrect ciphertext or decryption failure. The write-up underscores how tiny, human errors can undermine crypto modules and stresses the need for better input handling and automated testing in crypto libraries.</span><span class='zh'>在 PSpice 的 AES-256 加密实现中出现的一个拷贝粘贴错误打乱了原本的加密行为，可能导致密文错误或解密失败。文中强调微小的人为错误就能破坏加密模块的鲁棒性，并呼吁在加密库中加强输入处理和自动化测试。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ArXiv paper explores how vectorizing Verilog designs affects verification and synthesis, showing how bit-vector representations can impact equivalence checking and hardware synthesis results. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A revisitation of the Lowest Common Ancestor problem, presenting new insights or a survey of algorithms, with implications for tree data structures and graph queries.</span><span class='zh'>对最小公共祖先问题进行了再研究，给出新的见解或算法综述，影响树结构与图查询的实现。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.xoria.org/expr-stmt-c/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Side-Effectful Expressions in C (2023)</span><span class='zh'>C 语言中的副作用表达式（2023）</span></a><span class="dom">blog.xoria.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A technical exploration of side-effectful expressions in C, examining how side effects impact compiler optimizations and reasoning about code behavior.</span><span class='zh'>对 C 语言中副作用表达式的技术探讨，分析副作用如何影响编译器优化与代码行为推理，对正确性与性能具有不容忽视的影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A technical exploration of side-effectful expressions in C, examining how side effects impact compiler optimizations and reasoning about code behavior. It highlights the non-obvious implications for correctness and performance.</span><span class='zh'>对 C 语言中副作用表达式的技术探讨，分析副作用如何影响编译器优化与代码行为推理，对正确性与性能具有不容忽视的影响。</span></p>
